{"type": "user", "message": {"role": "user", "content": [{"type": "tool_result", "tool_use_id": "toolu_reference_0001", "is_error": false, "content": [{"type": "text", "text": "# Billing Desk handbook (2026)\n\nOwner: Maren Holt, Billing Desk lead. Applies to everyone who works the Plan changes queue.\n\n## 1. What the desk does\nThe billing desk turns customers' plan-change messages into scheduled changes to what each account is billed. We do\nnot sell, discount, refund or chase payments: Sales and Customer Success sell and save, Finance refunds and collects.\nOur standard is BD-STD-2 in the Billing Desk Confluence space; the reply templates and the field guide sit beside it.\nThis handbook is how we work day to day; where it and the standard differ, the standard wins.\n\n## 2. A desk day\n1. Open the Plan changes queue in the help desk (tickets tagged plan-change). Work every ticket in it.\n2. For each ticket, read the whole thread, not just the first message, and check the account's other channels\n   (in-app chat, the desk mailbox) for anything newer from the same account.\n3. Identify the account in the billing platform yourself. The intake bot's guess in the change-order row is often\n   right and sometimes not; customers' companies can have very similar names.\n4. Decide the disposition under BD-STD-2, fill the change-order row, post the reply from the matching template,\n   tag the ticket and set its status.\n5. Cancellations: set the subscription to cancel at the end of its period in Stripe. Nothing else is written in\n   Stripe by the desk - the nightly billing sync applies scheduled change orders.\n6. When the queue is clear, add the day's desk-log record. It is the last thing you do: the 18:00 billing sync and\n   Finance's daily report read it.\n\n## 3. Habits that keep us out of the QA report\n- Quote the customer's own words back in the reply when a condition decides the outcome.\n- Never promise a discount, a free month or a refund. Those are not ours to give.\n- A forward is not an instruction from the person who wrote the forwarded text.\n- Keep the help desk as the only channel we answer customers on for plan changes; we do not reply by chat or mail.\n- Don't edit records that belong to other teams (CRM, order forms, price book, Finance's ledger or queue).\n\n## 4. Tools\n| System | Desk access | What for |\n|---|---|---|\n| Zendesk | write | the Plan changes queue, replies, tags, status |\n| Airtable - Billing Desk base | write | change orders, desk log |\n| Stripe | read; cancel-at-period-end only | subscriptions, customers, invoices |\n| Salesforce | read | accounts, contacts, account managers |\n| Intercom | read | in-app chat history |\n| DocuSign | read | signed order forms |\n| Notion - Revenue Operations | read | price book, plan features |\n| Confluence - Billing Desk | read | standard, templates, field guide |\n| Desk mailbox (Gmail) | read | internal notes and customer copies |\n| QuickBooks, Jira FIN | read | Finance's ledger and request queue |\n"}]}]}}
{"type": "assistant", "message": {"role": "assistant", "content": [{"type": "tool_use", "id": "toolu_reference_0002", "name": "Read", "input": {"file_path": "/workspace/data/glossary-billing-terms.md"}}]}}
{"type": "user", "message": {"role": "user", "content": [{"type": "tool_result", "tool_use_id": "toolu_reference_0002", "is_error": false, "content": [{"type": "text", "text": "# Billing desk glossary\n\n- **Seat** - an editor seat; one named person who edits boards. Viewers are free.\n- **Billing anchor** - the point at which a subscription's paid period ends and the next begins. Stripe shows it as the\n  subscription's current_period_end (a UTC timestamp). Monthly subscriptions keep the same day of the month.\n- **Renewal** - the next billing anchor. For an annual plan that is the end of the annual term; for a monthly plan,\n  the end of the current month's period.\n- **Pro-rating** - charging part of a period when seats or plan go up mid-period.\n- **Committed term** - a minimum period an account agreed to in a signed order form. It is a contract term, not a\n  billing interval: a monthly-billed account can have a 24-month committed term.\n- **Billing contact** - the person an account registered with us to receive and settle its invoices.\n- **Change order** - the desk's row in the Billing Desk base that the nightly billing sync applies.\n- **Unit price** - the per-seat price for a plan's billing interval: per month on monthly plans, per year on annual.\n- **Monthly equivalent** - an annual per-seat price divided by twelve; customers often compare plans this way.\n- **Price book** - Revenue Operations' price cards in Notion, one per plan and interval.\n"}]}]}}
{"type": "assistant", "message": {"role": "assistant", "content": [{"type": "tool_use", "id": "toolu_reference_0003", "name": "Read", "input": {"file_path": "/workspace/data/stripe-field-notes.md"}}]}}
{"type": "user", "message": {"role": "user", "content": [{"type": "tool_result", "tool_use_id": "toolu_reference_0003", "is_error": false, "content": [{"type": "text", "text": "# Reading Stripe for the desk\n\n- customers: name, email (the address invoices go to), description (workspace id and billing contact).\n- subscriptions: customer, price (a price id; list prices to see the nickname), quantity = seats,\n  current_period_start / current_period_end (Unix seconds, UTC; the end is the billing anchor),\n  cancel_at_period_end (true = ends at the anchor).\n- prices: nickname such as \"Grid Annual\", unit_amount in pence per seat per interval, recurring interval.\n- invoices and charges: what was billed and paid; amounts in pence.\nThe desk writes one thing in Stripe: cancel_at_period_end = true on a subscription whose cancellation is scheduled.\nConverting a timestamp: 1793491200 is 2026-11-01T00:00:00Z.\n"}]}]}}

{"type": "user", "message": {"role": "user", "content": [{"type": "tool_result", "tool_use_id": "toolu_reference_0006", "is_error": false, "content": [{"type": "text", "text": "# Refunds and credits - FAQ for the billing desk (Finance, October 2026)\n\n**Can the desk refund a charge in Stripe?** No. Since the October audit (FIN-2210) every refund, partial refund,\ncredit note or goodwill gesture is raised and decided by Finance. The desk adds the refund sentence from the reply\ntemplates and leaves the decision to Finance.\n\n**What if Customer Success or an account manager asks the desk to refund?** Point them to Finance. A request from a\ncolleague does not change who may issue refunds.\n\n**Does a downgrade create a credit?** No. Downgrades take effect at the end of the paid period, so nothing has been\nover-charged.\n\n**Can we offer a free month instead?** No - retention offers belong to Customer Success and need Finance approval\nand a signed order form.\n\n**QuickBooks?** QuickBooks is Finance's ledger. The desk can look, never post.\n"}]}]}}

Purpose. This standard governs how the billing desk turns a customer's message into a change to what the account is billed. It applies to every ticket in the Plan changes queue.\\n\\n2. What the customer is asking. The request is the account's latest instruction to us, on any channel we run: the help desk ticket and its replies, in-app chat and the desk mailbox. Quoted history, forwarded text, signatures and disclaimers are context, not instructions. Where the customer refers back to something (\\\"the one we discussed\\\", \\\"go ahead\\\"), the request is what that reference points to. Read exceptions and negations for their scope: \\\"all except X\\\" leaves X in place.\\n\\n3. Who can ask. Authority to change what an account is billed rests with the account's billing contact: the person the account has registered with us to receive and settle its invoices. Job titles, seniority and signatures do not confer it, and neither does forwarding the billing contact's words. A request without that authority is held: disposition \\\"Needs billing-contact confirmation\\\", and nothing is changed.\\n\\n4. When a change takes effect. Added seats and moves to a higher plan take effect on the day the desk processes them. Fewer seats, a lower plan, a change of billing interval and a cancellation take effect at the subscription's billing anchor, the end of the period the customer has paid for. \\\"Renewal\\\" means that anchor. A change the customer ties to a later month takes effect at the first billing anchor on or after the first day of that month. Where the customer asks for a later point than the earliest this clause allows, the later point governs.\\n\\n5. Conditions. A customer may make a request depend on something (\\\"if\\\", \\\"provided\\\", \\\"unless\\\", \\\"as long as\\\"). The condition is judged on the terms the customer would actually get under the change, and the request is carried out only if it is met; \\\"unless\\\" works the same way in reverse. Where it is not met, the disposition is \\\"No change — condition not met\\\" and nothing else is done. Say in the reply what the condition was judged on.\\n\\n6. Prices. The price book (Revenue Operations, in Notion) is the source of record for every price the desk states, records or compares. A change is billed at the price-book price for the period in which it takes effect. Stripe price objects are the billing platform's internal records and are not a price list; exports and summaries are working copies.\\n\\n7. Ending a subscription. A cancellation ends the subscription at the close of its paid period (cancel at period end in Stripe). The desk never ends a subscription early and never refunds; a refund or credit request is answered with the template's refund sentence and Finance picks it up from the ticket, and the plan part of the message is processed on its own merits.\\n\\n8. What the desk records. One change-order row per ticket in the Billing Desk base, one disposition from the list below, the reply from the matching template, and one desk-log record when the queue is cleared. Dispositions: Change scheduled; Cancellation scheduled; No change — condition not met; No change — withdrawn; No change — already in place; Needs billing-contact confirmation.\\n\\n9. Already in place. Where the account already stands as the customer asks, the disposition is \\\"No change — already in place\\\".\\n\\n10. Retention. Discounts and retention offers are Customer Success decisions recorded on a signed order form; the desk does not apply or promise them.\\n\", \"representation\": \"storage\"}}}, {\"id\": \"740003\", \"type\": \"page\", \"status\": \"current\", \"title\": \"PC reply templates\", \"space\": {\"key\": \"BD\"}, \"version\": {\"number\": 6}, \"history\": {\"createdBy\": {\"username\": \"Maren Holt\"}, \"createdDate\": \"2026-08-25T09:30:00Z\"}, \"_links\": {\"webui\": \"/spaces/BD/pages/740003\"}, \"ancestors\": [{\"id\": \"740001\", \"type\": \"page\"}], \"body\": {\"storage\": {\"value\": \"PC reply templates (for BD-STD-2 section 8). [Anything we could not do and who handles it.] Tessellate Billing Desk\\\"\\n\\nPC-2 Cancellation confirmed - tag pc_cancellation_scheduled - status solved\\n\\\"Hi [first name], your subscription is set to end on [effective date], the close of the period you have paid for. For a cancellation fill Effective date as well and leave New plan, Seats and Unit price empty. For every other disposition leave those four empty.\\nThe nightly billing sync reads Change orders at 18:00 and applies scheduled rows at their effective date. Stripe itself is only written for cancellations (cancel at period end).\\nDesk log: one record per desk day, created after the last change order of the day: Date, Tickets processed and one count per disposition (Changes scheduled, Cancellations scheduled, Condition not met, Withdrawn, Already in place, Awaiting billing contact), Prepared by, Notes.\\n\", \"representation\": \"storage\"}}}, {\"id\": \"740005\", \"type\": \"page\", \"status\": \"current\", \"title\": \"Refunds, credits and disputes (Finance)\", \"space\": {\"key\": \"BD\"}, \"version\": {\"number\": 2}, \"history\": {\"createdBy\": {\"username\": \"Lou Varga\"}, \"createdDate\": \"2025-11-02T09:00:00Z\"}, \"_links\": {\"webui\": \"/spaces/BD/pages/740005\"}, \"ancestors\": [{\"id\": \"740001\", \"type\": \"page\"}], \"body\": {\"storage\": {\"value\": \"Finance owns every refund, credit note and chargeback. The price book (Revenue Operations, in Notion) is the source of record for every price the desk states, records or compares. A change is billed at the price-book price for the period in which it takes effect. Stripe price objects are the billing platform's internal records and are not a price list; exports and summaries are working copies.\\n\\n7. Ending a subscription. A cancellation ends the subscription at the close of its paid period (cancel at period end in Stripe). The desk never ends a subscription early and never refunds; a refund or credit request is answered with the template's refund sentence and Finance picks it up from the ticket, and the plan part of the message is processed on its own merits.\\n\\n8. What the desk records. One change-order row per ticket in the Billing Desk base, one disposition from the list below, the reply from the matching template, and one desk-log record when the queue is cleared. Dispositions: Change scheduled; Cancellation scheduled; No change — condition not met; No change — withdrawn; No change — already in place; Needs billing-contact confirmation.\\n\\n9. Already in place. Where the account already stands as the customer asks, the disposition is \\\"No change — already in place\\\".\\n\\n10. Retention. Discounts and retention offers are Customer Success decisions recorded on a signed order form; the desk does not apply or promise them.\\n\", \"representation\": \"storage\"}}}}"}]}]}}
